# Agents · R · part 4

1,167 entries, reader to README. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reader**: Authoring subagent for paper-deep-read products. Dispatch with PARAMETERS ONLY (mode, key, paths, figure channel, float ids to read, product paths) — never paste workflow text; the agent reads contracts from its plugin directory. Executes one mode per dispatch (machine | tldr | human | full, optional --code) and returns a report: product paths, route, figures read per channel, degraded figures, reflow flags, [uncertain] count. Use when the dispatcher wants heavy reading/authoring moved out of the main context. ([Bryce-Peng/paper-deep-read](https://github.com/Bryce-Peng/paper-deep-read/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **reader**: Read-only recon: finds files, symbols and usages, and distills long files, logs or diffs that would flood the main session. Returns file:line pointers with the shortest excerpts and names what it did not search. Not for edits, running commands or judgments. ([bug3/yapus](https://github.com/bug3/yapus/blob/HEAD/plugins/delegator/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Cheap, fast reader for any project; the first choice for reading and sweeps. Use for "where is X", "what does this file do", "list every place that does Y", digging through a log, sweeping a codebase or summarising a long document, when the answer is a fact found in files. Read-only. Not for judgment, security review, design decisions or writing code; those go to a stronger agent. ([CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit](https://github.com/CAdidas333/claude-code-starter-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads a pull request and answers one question about it for the review workflow. ([danielbacsur/skills](https://github.com/danielbacsur/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Reads large outputs (logs, code sweeps, long docs, query results) and returns only the summary the orchestrator asked for. Edits nothing; read-only terminal. Use it whenever the orchestrator would have to read a lot to keep a little. Runs on haiku to save tokens. ([danielsaed/sdd-base](https://github.com/danielsaed/sdd-base/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **reader**: Use for read-only exploration when more than three files are involved: code, docs, schemas, logs. Returns findings with file:line and no edits. Skip for a single-file lookup. ([Deluha/council-of-elrond](https://github.com/Deluha/council-of-elrond/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Finds the act in a register's views and transcribes it, doubts marked. ([devohmycode/Probandia](https://github.com/devohmycode/Probandia/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **reader**: Finds and reads code and log files in Znalica on a cheap model and returns path:line with the few lines that matter. Use for "where is X", "what does Y do", "who calls Z", "which tests failed in this run log" instead of reading whole files yourself. ([fmoric/Znalica](https://github.com/fmoric/Znalica/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **reader**: Thesis SLS row 3a. Reads one included paper and drafts its record (five Cs, quality, extracted fields, concepts, with pages). Dispatched by the sls-3-reading skill, one per paper. ([GarroteSantiago/Thesis](https://github.com/GarroteSantiago/Thesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: A reader test. Give it only a finished page the user will read and act on; it reports where the page fails a reader who isn't technical. It checks whether the page can be acted on, not whether it's true. ([GoalPower-ai/goalpower-for-claude](https://github.com/GoalPower-ai/goalpower-for-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: A read-only agent (Read, Grep and Glob only) for the briefs that read a stranger's text. /ap:bug-reports starts it with the bug-triage or the why-missed brief of the ap plugin. - **reader**: Braidry Full-text reader for one step of a Braidry braid. Started only by the Braidry plugin, with a ticket as its whole prompt. ([heuriscode/braidry-plugin](https://github.com/heuriscode/braidry-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/braidry/agents/reader.md))
- **reader**: Read-only code reader. Use it when answering a question means reading more than about 3 files or 300 lines that you will not edit (how does X work, where is Y, what calls Z). Read-only — returns a structured reader-experience report grounded in the screenshot + JSX + schema + framework reader-intent doc, never edits files. ([pranav-9/concall-alpha](https://github.com/pranav-9/concall-alpha/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reader-eye.md))
